# IRGS Shared Content Library — Maturation R2

**Status:** Complete proposed qualitative dossiers for the remaining 192 held families; no automatic promotion.
**Dependencies:** Core v3.2 (unchanged), SCL v1.0 / @0.2 content identities, preserved source/genre adapters, R1 supplement for 36 families.

## Scope and authority

This is an additive catalogue qualification supplement. The 51 currently selected contracts remain selected. The 228 original source-backed holds **remain held**: 36 have R1 drafts and 192 have R2 drafts. Completion of a written qualitative contract is neither activation nor numerical calibration. Source facts and editorial design proposals are marked distinctly below. No campaign migration or retroactive changes are authorized.

## Portable contract by semantic type

### `armor`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ASSET/K-ACTOR.  
**Definition:** Protective construction fitted to specific physical regions.  
**Activation gate:** Actual constructed component, wearability, coverage/gaps, material, fit and compatible protection model.  
**Effect boundary:** Protection only where a supported material layer intercepts a relevant channel; no stacked full ratings.

### `behavior`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ACTOR/K-POP/K-INFO.  
**Definition:** Behavioral/ecological template.  
**Activation gate:** Real organism/cohort, internal needs, sensory knowledge, goals, reachable targets and current condition.  
**Effect boundary:** Observed autonomous behavior follows capability; descriptor cannot spawn animals or grant competence.

### `crisis`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-TIME/K-ORG/K-ASSET/K-POP.  
**Definition:** Multi-process scenario descriptor without autonomous spawn authority.  
**Activation gate:** Specific initiating cause, geography, actors, timeline, mechanisms, observation and termination.  
**Effect boundary:** Effects apply through existing systems only; no automatic population removal or infinite crises.

### `equipment`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ASSET/K-WORK/K-STOCK.  
**Definition:** Physical equipment configuration and its usable operating mode.  
**Activation gate:** Existing configured asset, function-specific connection, operator/setup, accessible energy/inputs and condition.  
**Effect boundary:** Supported delivery consumes time/inputs and uses actual task; nominal equipment listing provides no service.

### `flow`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-NET/K-STOCK/K-INFO/K-POP.  
**Definition:** Planned movement or capacity allocation across real links.  
**Activation gate:** Source/destination, type/quantity, rights, route, window, provider resources and acceptance.  
**Effect boundary:** Do not count in-transit payload at destination; forecasts/reports do not transfer ownership or resources.

### `habitation`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ACTOR/K-POP/K-ASSET/K-NET.  
**Definition:** Habitation arrangement and service-demand view.  
**Activation gate:** Actual people/groups, location, housing, sanitary/thermal/water conditions, support and capacity.  
**Effect boundary:** No new residents, beds, safety or supplies from selecting a habitation category.

### `hazard`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ASSET/K-ACTOR/K-POP/K-TIME.  
**Definition:** Potential exposure/effect channel, not an automatically active event.  
**Activation gate:** Actual source/intensity/location/path, exposed target, protection, onset/interval and matching consequence model.  
**Effect boundary:** Only causal supported exposures change condition; detection or category selection is not injury.

### `information_content`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-INFO/K-ASSET.  
**Definition:** Informational content, optionally embodied in separately recorded physical media.  
**Activation gate:** Known provenance/version/time, actual observer/recipient/access, validation status; physical copy only if instantiated.  
**Effect boundary:** Knowledge transfer updates recipient information; physical copy uses an asset and does not clone authority.

### `institution`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ORG/K-ACTOR/K-INFO.  
**Definition:** Institutional role or office as an organizational template.  
**Activation gate:** Actual founding/authorization, personnel, mandate, access/resources, procedures and accountable records.  
**Effect boundary:** Office name does not create statutory authority, trained staff, stock or functioning service.

### `link`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-NET/K-ASSET/K-TIME.  
**Definition:** Connecting channel or route between real endpoints.  
**Activation gate:** Named endpoints, medium, supported path, mode compatibility, access/capacity, delay and maintenance.  
**Effect boundary:** Movement or transmission only over traversable working link; reservations are not receipts.

### `method`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ACTOR/K-WORK.  
**Definition:** Physical action method, not an owned weapon or an additional turn.  
**Activation gate:** Feasible limb contact, actual actor ability and available commitment.  
**Effect boundary:** Only the actor action already declared; no independent inventory, attack or damage.

### `movement_activity`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ACTOR/K-POP/K-NET/K-TIME.  
**Definition:** Movement of existing people or cohorts.  
**Activation gate:** Actual identified members/counts, source/destination, feasible movement, consent/authorization, means and support.  
**Effect boundary:** Transfer the same people between locations; no duplicate arrivals, deaths or free carrying capacity.

### `natural_delivery`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ACTOR/K-POP.  
**Definition:** Anatomy-dependent physical delivery method.  
**Activation gate:** Actual organism, relevant organ and position, action commitment, physical effect plus any separate exposure carrier.  
**Effect boundary:** No attack, restraint, venom or free action without actual delivery and named species trait.

### `program`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-WORK/K-TIME/K-ORG.  
**Definition:** Project-program outcome/acceptance template.  
**Activation gate:** Authorized deliverable and owner, actual tasks, inputs, personnel, staged prerequisites and acceptance evidence.  
**Effect boundary:** Progress is not functioning capacity until commissioned/accepted; no duplicate costs.

### `service`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-NET/K-WORK/K-ASSET/K-STOCK.  
**Definition:** Useful recurring output allocated from physically capable providers.  
**Activation gate:** Defined output unit and quality, provider bundle, demand endpoints, link access and constraints.  
**Effect boundary:** Accepted useful output only, bounded by shared throughput, reserved commitments, buffers and input stocks.

### `site`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ASSET/K-ORG/K-INFO.  
**Definition:** Location/site archetype with possible installed systems.  
**Activation gate:** Actual site/location, layout/rights, access, installed assets, staff and measured conditions.  
**Effect boundary:** An archetype can host facilities; plausible contents are not instantiated or replenished.

### `stock`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-STOCK.  
**Definition:** Fungible goods classification only within exact compatible lot specification.  
**Activation gate:** Specified lot, actual positive quantity and unit, accessible location, owner/custodian and eligibility.  
**Effect boundary:** Conserved debit/credit/consumption, quarantine and quality updates; template cannot create stock.

### `storage_system`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ASSET/K-STOCK.  
**Definition:** Physical containment/holding interface.  
**Activation gate:** Specific container/zone, geometry/capacity, material compatibility, seal/access, actual intake and records.  
**Effect boundary:** Stock in a store is recorded once at its actual position; capacity is not stored quantity.

### `weapon`

**Single owner/read interface:** K-ASSET/K-ACTOR/K-STOCK.  
**Definition:** Constructed implement with selectable physical modes.  
**Activation gate:** Actual item, usable condition, grip/hands, reach/geometry, selected mode and actor capability.  
**Effect boundary:** Use a single selected action mode; a cut/thrust is not a second item or free action.

## Individually reconciled families

### 001. Pack hunter — `SCL:PBC-BIO-FL-CRT-002@0.2`

**Source identity:** FL:CRT-002  
**Status:** proposed_qualitative_contract_not_adopted | **Type:** `behavior` | **Parent owner(s):** `K-ACTOR/K-POP/K-INFO`

**Source facts:** The original neutral rows and source-bound records are preserved in the JSON appendix. No extra numeric values inferred.

**Purpose — proposed:** Behavioral/ecological template — Pack hunter

**Required actual binding — proposed:** Real organism/cohort, internal needs, sensory knowledge, goals, reachable targets and current condition

**Entry-specific qualification — proposed:** Species ecology and observable behavior for Pack hunter

**Permitted effect and owner — proposed:** Observed autonomous behavior follows capability; descriptor cannot spawn animals or grant competence

**Negative boundary — proposed:** The Pack hunter template cannot summon new creatures or grant free actions

**Runtime and interval — proposed:** Resolve feasibility from actual state, qualify source and actor, allocate compatible capacity/resources once, execute the action or process, then record measured effect separately from reports.

**Reassessment trigger — proposed:** Use a source/selected-profile-defined activation or threshold event and relevant work/transport time. No universal autonomous ticking, reset at scale shift or re-trigger on read.

**Condition and support — proposed:** Condition, maintenance, restocking, repair, calibration and operator requirements remain actual asset/service/work responsibilities, not granted by lookup.

**Adapter ownership — proposed:** Portability applies to content identity, interfaces, and qualitative constraints. No Fantasy Threat/Defeat, medicine effectiveness, injury timing, structural loading, power, heat or production rate is universalized.

**Positive acceptance specification:** Set actual creatures with needs, perception, terrain and goals, and advance their supported independent behavior. For this family additionally verify: Species ecology and observable behavior for Pack hunter.

**Negative / regression specification:** A behavior label without real creatures or means cannot create a hunting pack or repeated attacks. Family-specific non-effect: The Pack hunter template cannot summon new creatures or grant free actions.

**Uncalibrated dependency fields:** species/ecology; actual members; needs/senses; behavioral causes; reproduction/renewal where used.

**Promotion:** Retain the current hold until source review, native adapter validation, executable acceptance tests, new per-definition checksum/ID revision, and explicit adoption.

**Permitted effect and owner — proposed:** No attack, restraint, venom or free action without actual delivery and named species trait

**Negative boundary — proposed:** A Bite descriptor does not automatically injure, grapple, poison or strike twice

**Runtime and interval — proposed:** Resolve feasibility from actual state, qualify source and actor, allocate compatible capacity/resources once, execute the action or process, then record measured effect separately from reports.

**Reassessment trigger — proposed:** Use a source/selected-profile-defined activation or threshold event and relevant work/transport time. No universal autonomous ticking, reset at scale shift or re-trigger on read.

**Positive acceptance specification:** Give an existing creature the specified organ, effective position and action; check delivery before any separate toxic/exposure effect. For this family additionally verify: Anatomical organ and positioned delivery for Bite.

**Uncalibrated dependency fields:** species anatomy; delivery prerequisites; effect/physiology profile; costs/timing where relevant.
